*GPT message: I have typed "testing keyboard" into the search field in the Messages app, and the fcitx5 keyboard is active. The app has not crashed.

The bug report specifies that the crash occurs when the preferred input method is one supported by an addon (in this case, RIME, which is for Chinese input). So far, I have been using the English layout. To better match the conditions of the bug report, I need to switch to the Chinese input method provided by the RIME addon.

The keyboard has a language switch key, which is the most direct way to do this.

Suggestion:
```json
[
  {
    "action": "click",
    "feature": "org.fcitx.fcitx5.android:id/button_lang"
  }
]
```
